review_text: This was our first time eating here, but had been to some of the other restaurants of this group. I'm sad that we waited to try this place. Everything we ate was AMAZING. We ordered the pimento hushpuppies with blueberry pepper jelly to start. They were light and had so much flavor. I ordered the meat-and-three pork chop plate with collards, Mac and cheese and coleslaw slaw. The pork chops had a delicious, well-seasoned coating and were very tender. The collards were smoky and tasted fresh. The cole slaw was good and had sliced red onion in it, which was a nice addition. The Mac and cheese was creamy and comforting. Exceptional mac and cheese. My husband ordered the Beerger. The beer cheese was really good (he gave me a fry dipped in it). It was a good looking burger and someone had even put the beer mustard on in the shape of a smiley face. The burger was cooked perfectly and had a lot of flavor. The fries were also better than the norm. The service was wonderful, the manager made a point to greet us and was super sweet. We will definitely make a point to visit any time we're in the area.

review_id: 1155
entity_slug: lunas-eat-and-drink-orange-beach

rating: 5
review_text: We found this spot by searching Google reviews. We went around 2pm on a Sunday and there was no wait. The building and courtyard were so peaceful and home-like. Shantel was our waitress, and she gave us excellent recommendations.  She was very kind and welcoming.  We had the pimento bites appetizer,  but I was disappointed. It was more like a corn fritter. Nothing like I expected.  However, the crab and artichoke appetizer was so good we had to buy one to go!!!!! Highly recommend! My family and I all got the meat and three. Between us, we had catfish, grilled fish of the day, and Mahi Mahi. We also had mac and cheese, green beans, mashed sweet potatoes,  roasted red potatoes,  collard greens, fries, and cornbread. We were NOT let down. Everything was well seasoned and cooked well. We will put this on our list to visit again.

rating: 5
body: The absolute BEST restaurant in Orange Beach. We stopped in last month and fell in love with the service, food, beers, and atmosphere. We promised we would be back with our dog since they are pet friendly, so we returned a month later to dine on their beautiful patio and watch some LSU/Bama football. The service is amazing, we love just chatting with the servers and bartenders. The epitome of Southern hospitality. The food? I would do the three hour drive every weekend just to get that Cajun Mahi entree. I sub the grits for mashed potatoes and the Mac n cheese. The beers are always changing and there are SO many to choose from. We are Luna's fans for life!

--- entity_reviews [row 3 of 5] ---
id: 80420b5f-e8c1-484c-ba29-78da4229f618
entity_slug: lunas-eat-and-drink-orange-beach
reviewer_name: Nan Rust
rating: 5
body: This was our first time eating here, but had been to some of the other restaurants of this group. I'm sad that we waited to try this place. Everything we ate was AMAZING. We ordered the pimento hushpuppies with blueberry pepper jelly to start. They were light and had so much flavor. I ordered the meat-and-three pork chop plate with collards, Mac and cheese and coleslaw slaw. The pork chops had a delicious, well-seasoned coating and were very tender. The collards were smoky and tasted fresh. The cole slaw was good and had sliced red onion in it, which was a nice addition. The Mac and cheese was creamy and comforting. Exceptional mac and cheese. My husband ordered the Beerger. The beer cheese was really good (he gave me a fry dipped in it). It was a good looking burger and someone had even put the beer mustard on in the shape of a smiley face. The burger was cooked perfectly and had a lot of flavor. The fries were also better than the norm. The service was wonderful, the manager made a point to greet us and was super sweet. We will definitely make a point to visit any time we're in the area.

reviewer_name: Demi Something
rating: 5
body: Food? Outstanding. Everything was good. Meatballs are famous for a reason, and they are HUGE. Mac & cheese is somehow even more delicious than it sounds.

We came on New Years Eve, during a large lunch crowd surge. During our wait, we walked over and grabbed a coffee from Buzzcats. The waiting list is visible VIA a text link,  and once your table is ready, you receive a text & have 10 minutes to get back to claim your table.

We had incredibly warm and high caliber service from Ronald, he is clearly an expert in the bartending / serving industry! Despite busy crowds, he was friendly, efficient, and knowledgeable about the menu and drinks.We will definitely be making a point to come back each time we visit Orange Beach (probably multiple times per visit ☺️)!

entity_slug: lunas-eat-and-drink-orange-beach
reviewer_name: April Squire
rating: 5
body: We found this spot by searching Google reviews. We went around 2pm on a Sunday and there was no wait. The building and courtyard were so peaceful and home-like. Shantel was our waitress, and she gave us excellent recommendations.  She was very kind and welcoming.  We had the pimento bites appetizer,  but I was disappointed. It was more like a corn fritter. Nothing like I expected.  However, the crab and artichoke appetizer was so good we had to buy one to go!!!!! Highly recommend! My family and I all got the meat and three. Between us, we had catfish, grilled fish of the day, and Mahi Mahi. We also had mac and cheese, green beans, mashed sweet potatoes,  roasted red potatoes,  collard greens, fries, and cornbread. We were NOT let down. Everything was well seasoned and cooked well. We will put this on our list to visit again.
